It’s just been coming into my head, and I thought I should probably talk to someone.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been having these thoughts about not wanting to wake up?\n\nPatient: I guess… it’s been happening enough lately that it’s what made me come in. I keep catching myself thinking maybe it’d be easier if I just didn’t wake up. I don’t have a plan or anything like that, I promise—it’s more like the thought pops up, and then I feel bad for even having it.\n\nDoctor: How is your mood been lately?\n\nPatient: It’s been… pretty low, I guess. I mean, I’m trying to keep it together, but after that burst of energy wore off, it felt like somebody just pulled the plug on me. I’d sit at the table staring at my coffee and just feel empty… and kind of ashamed, honestly. I don’t know if that makes sense.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this low mood?\n\nPatient: It’s been… I’d say about three months now, since that crash after the energy wore off. I kept thinking it would pass, so I didn’t want to make too big a deal of it, but yeah… it’s kind of been hanging around that long.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any decrease in interest or pleasure in activities that you normally enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess I have noticed that. I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but the stuff that usually helps me unwind just hasn’t been doing much. Fishing, looking through my vinyl records, tailgating… even checking on my home-brew, it all started feeling more like chores than things I actually wanted to do. Is that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your movement or activity level, such as feeling restless or sluggish?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure… I haven’t noticed anything big like that. Maybe I move a little slower some days, but I don’t know if that’s just being tired or me overthinking it. I wouldn’t want to say yes if it’s not really, you know, a thing.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your weight lately?\n\nPatient: A little, I guess… nothing dramatic, I don’t think. But my jeans have been fitting looser lately. Most days I kind of have to remind myself to eat, and supper just sits there looking like… more trouble than it’s worth. Is that the sort of change you mean?\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling guilty about things you've done or haven't done?\n\nPatient: Yeah… some. I don’t want to make it sound like I’m sitting around beating myself up all day, but after that burst of energy wore off, it was like somebody pulled the plug on me.
